## Analyzer Architecture Summary

trainer-operator (also known as\: Trainer operator, Kubeflow Trainer operator) is a Kubernetes component. managing 1 CRD(s)\: Trainer. deploying 4 workload(s) (controller-manager, kubeflow-trainer-controller-manager, kubeflow-trainer-controller-manager, kubeflow-trainer-controller-manager) with 4 container(s). watching 4 resource type(s). protected by 2 network policy/policies. defining 1 API spec type(s) across 5 struct(s) with 14 total fields. with 50 operator config constants (7 image references). integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations. kustomize overlays\: config/crd, config/default, config/manager, config/manifests, config/network-policy, config/prometheus, config/rbac, config/scorecard. mounts 1 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: kubeflow-trainer-config.
